1. **Side effects:** Antipsychotic medications can have significant side effects, such as weight gain, increased risk of diabetes, and increased risk of blood clots. If you experience any of these side effects, it may be worth discussing with your doctor.
2. **Dose adjustment:** Your doctor may need to adjust your medication dose to minimize side effects.
3. **New medications:** If you're taking a new medication, it may interact with your existing antipsychotic medication, leading to increased side effects.
4. **Changes in your treatment plan:** Your doctor may need to adjust your treatment plan to ensure you're receiving the best possible care.

**Reasons not to stop your medication:**

1. **Safety:** Stopping your medication abruptly can lead to a rebound effect, where your symptoms return or worsen.
2. **Depression and anxiety:** Antipsychotic medications can help manage symptoms of depression and anxiety.
